          if (!data) { toast(uiT('bgImageBad'), 'err', 2500); return; }
          BG_IMAGE = data; BGFX_STYLE = 'image'; BGFX_ON = true;
          wv('UI_BGIMG:' + data);
          wv('UI_BGSTYLE:image');
          wv('UI_BGFX:1');
          bgfxApply(); renderBgOpts();
          toast(uiT('bgImageSet'), 'ok', 2000);
        });
      };
      rd.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  })();


  bgfxApply();

  var searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
  /* 性能：每敲一个字就重建整片卡片（含重新加载封面图）会明显卡手
     这里做个防抖，停手 140ms 后再渲染一次。 */
  var searchTimer = 0;
  searchInput.addEventListener('input', function(){
    var v = this.value.trim();
    if (searchTimer) { clearTimeout(searchTimer); }
    searchTimer = setTimeout(function(){
      searchTimer = 0;
      if (v === SEARCH) { return; }
      SEARCH = v;
      renderGrid();
    }, 140);
  });

  document.getElementById('secFilter').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn = e.target.closest('button');
    if (!btn) return;
    setFilter(btn.getAttribute('data-filter'));
  });

  function onHeroBuy(){
    if (hasLic(HERO_GAME)) {
      openDetail(HERO_GAME);
      setTimeout(function(){ jumpToInject(); }, 300);
    } else {
      renewGame(null, HERO_GAME);
    }
  }

  function renewGame(ev, g){
    if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
    /* 把“当前点的那个游戏”带进 Renew 页：价格、输入框前缀、封面/订阅时间都靠它 */
    var code = g || CURRENT_GAME || HERO_GAME || '';
    RETURN_GAME = CURRENT_GAME || '';
    if (typeof uiOpenRenew === 'function') uiOpenRenew(code);
  }

  function uiToggleTheme(){
    var cur = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';
    var next = 'dark';
    if (cur === 'dark') next = 'light';
    if (typeof uiSetTheme === 'function') uiSetTheme(next);
  }


  window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', function(e){
    var msg = e.data;
    if (typeof msg !== 'string') return;

    if (msg.indexOf('LICENSES:') === 0) {
      try {
        LICENSES = JSON.parse(msg.slice(9));
        ACTIVE_GAME = LICENSES.active || ACTIVE_GAME;
        PAUSE_PENDING = {};             /* 服务端状态已回来，清掉「处理中」 */
        renderHero();
        renderGrid();
        renderQuickplay();
        renderDownloads();
        updateProfileBadges();          /* 订阅变了，PRO 徽标跟着变 */
        if (CURRENT_GAME) {
          var owned = hasLic(CURRENT_GAME);
          document.getElementById('subSection').hidden = !owned;
          document.getElementById('injectSection').hidden = !owned;
          if (owned) updateSubDisplay(licOf(CURRENT_GAME));
          renderDetailHeroButtons(CURRENT_GAME);
        }
      } catch (err) {}
    }
    else if (msg === 'PAUSE_DENIED') {
      PAUSE_PENDING = {};
      /* 宿主检测到游戏进程在跑：视为已启动，暂停按钮从此刻起保持禁用 */
      if (CURRENT_GAME) { LAUNCHED[CURRENT_GAME] = true; refreshPauseUi(CURRENT_GAME); }
      toast(uiT('pauseBlocked'), 'err', 3500);
    }
    else if (msg === 'PAUSE_ERR') {
      PAUSE_PENDING = {};
      if (CURRENT_GAME) refreshPauseUi(CURRENT_GAME);
      toast(uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3000);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('PAUSE_ERR:') === 0) {
      PAUSE_PENDING = {};
      if (CURRENT_GAME) refreshPauseUi(CURRENT_GAME);
      toast(msg.slice(10) || uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3500);
    }
    else if (msg === 'REQ_OK') {
      reqSetBusy(false); closeGameReq();
      toast(uiT('reqOk'), 'ok', 3000);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REQ_ERR:') === 0) {
      reqSetBusy(false);
      var rc = msg.slice(8);
      if (rc === 'GAME') { document.getElementById('reqGame').classList.add('is-err'); toast(uiT('reqErrGame'), 'err', 2500); }
      else if (rc === 'RATE') { toast(uiT('reqErrRate'), 'err', 3000); }
      else if (rc === 'AUTH') { toast(uiT('errAuth'), 'err', 3000); }
      else if (rc.indexOf('TEXT:') === 0) { toast(rc.slice(5) || uiT('accErrFail'), 'err', 3500); }
      else { toast(uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3000); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('TASK_BEGIN:') === 0) {
      var bg = msg.slice(11);
      if (bg && GAMES[bg]) { sbTaskShow(bg, uiT('injecting')); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('TASK_PROG:') === 0) {
      var pp = msg.slice(10).split(':');
      var pv = parseInt(pp[1], 10);
      if (isFinite(pv)) { injectOnProgress(pp[0], pv); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('TASK_STAGE:') === 0) {
      var ss2 = msg.slice(11).split(':');
      if (ss2[1] === 'extract') { sbTaskStage(uiT('extracting')); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('INJECT_OK:') === 0) {
      toast(uiT('injectOk'), 'ok');
      injectOnOk(msg.slice(10));
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('RUNS:') === 0) {
      /* 启动成功后宿主把新的运行次数发回来，就地更新，不用重开程序 */
      var rParts = msg.slice(5).split('|');
      var rCode = rParts[0];
      if (GAMES[rCode]) {
        GAMES[rCode].runs = parseInt(rParts[1], 10) || 0;
        GAMES[rCode].runsWeek = parseInt(rParts[2], 10) || 0;
      }
      if (typeof gpopRefreshStats === 'function') gpopRefreshStats(rCode);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('GAMESUPD:') === 0) {
      /* 宿主后台拉到新的 games.txt：GAMESUPD:<games json>\n<顺序 csv>，就地换上 */
      var gBody = msg.slice(9);
      var nl = gBody.indexOf('\n');
      var gJson = (nl >= 0) ? gBody.slice(0, nl) : gBody;
      var gOrder = (nl >= 0) ? gBody.slice(nl + 1) : '';
      if (typeof applyGamesUpdate === 'function') applyGamesUpdate(gJson, gOrder);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('INJECT_ERR:') === 0) {
      var parts = msg.split(':');
      var reason = '';
      if (parts.length > 2) reason = parts.slice(2).join(':');
      if (CURRENT_GAME) LAUNCHING[CURRENT_GAME] = false;
      toast(uiT('injectFail') + uiErrText(reason), 'err');
      cancelInject();
      sbTaskFinish('err', uiT('injectFail') + uiErrText(reason), 7000);
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('RENEW_OK:') === 0) {
      if (typeof uiRenewResult === 'function') {
        uiRenewResult(true, msg.slice(9));
      }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('RENEW_ERR:') === 0) {
      if (typeof uiRenewResult === 'function') {
        uiRenewResult(false, msg.slice(10));
      }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REVIEWS_RAW:') === 0) {
      /* 宿主把网站返回的原文 JSON 直接转过来，这里自己 parse */
      try {
        var rv = JSON.parse(msg.slice(12));
        REVIEWS = (rv && rv.reviews) || [];
      } catch (err) { REVIEWS = []; }
      REV_LIMIT = REV_FIRST;        /* 新数据回来：回到首屏 3 条 */
      renderReviews();
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REVIEWS_ERR:') === 0) {
      REVIEWS = [];
      var revBox = document.getElementById('revList');
      var revEmpty = document.getElementById('revEmpty');
      if (revBox) revBox.innerHTML = '';
      if (revEmpty) { revEmpty.textContent = uiT('revErrLoad'); revEmpty.hidden = false; }
    }
    else if (msg === 'REVIEW_OK') {
      revSetBusy(false);
      closeReviewModal();
      toast(uiT('revOk'), 'ok', 3500);
      REVIEWS = null;
      wv('REVIEWS');          /* 立刻再拉一次（新评价要审核过后才会出现） */
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('REVIEW_ERR:') === 0) {
      revSetBusy(false);
      var rc = msg.slice(11);
      if (rc === 'SHORT')        { toast(uiT('revErrShort'), 'err', 2500); }
      else if (rc === 'PENDING') { toast(uiT('revPending'), 'err', 3500); }
      else if (rc === 'AUTH')    { toast(uiT('errAuth'), 'err', 3000); }
      else if (rc.indexOf('TEXT:') === 0) { toast(rc.slice(5) || uiT('accErrFail'), 'err', 3500); }
      else                       { toast(uiT('errNet'), 'err', 3000); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('MYGROUP:') === 0) {
      /* 网站回传的实时组：正常用户 = release，管理员 = admin */
      var gp = msg.slice(8).split('|');
      if (gp[0]) MY_GROUP = gp[0];
      if (gp[1]) MY_ROLE  = gp[1];
      updateProfileGroup();
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('ADMIN_RAW:') === 0) {
      var arest  = msg.slice(10);
      var acolon = arest.indexOf(':');
      var aact   = (acolon >= 0) ? arest.slice(0, acolon) : arest;
      var abody  = (acolon >= 0) ? arest.slice(acolon + 1) : '';
      var ajson = null, aok = false, amsg = '';
      try {
        ajson = JSON.parse(abody);
        aok   = !!(ajson && ajson.ok);
        amsg  = (ajson && (ajson.msg || ajson.error)) || '';
      } catch (e) {}

      /* 列表类请求：成功就落数据重绘；失败把错误显示在列表区域里，
         **绝不再自动重试**（否则失败 → 重试 → 又失败，会把界面打成死循环） */
      if (aact === 'users') {
        ADMIN_ERR = aok ? '' : (amsg || uiT('adminFailed'));
        if (aok) { ADMIN_USERS = (ajson && ajson.users) || []; }
        renderAdminUsers();
      } else if (aact === 'tickets') {
        ADMIN_ERR = aok ? '' : (amsg || uiT('adminFailed'));
        if (aok) { ADMIN_TICKETS = (ajson && ajson.tickets) || []; }
        renderAdminTickets();
      } else if (!aok) {
        toast(amsg || uiT('adminFailed'), 'err', 3500);
      } else {
        if (amsg) { toast(amsg, 'ok', 2500); }
        /* 只有「操作成功」才回头刷新列表 */
        if (aact.indexOf('ticket') === 0) { wv('ADMIN:tickets'); wv('ADMIN:users'); }
        else { wv('ADMIN:users'); }
      }
    }
    else if (msg === 'SUBS_ERR') {
      /* 网站那边订阅拉不到（比如新版 api/auth.php 还没上传）——只提醒一次 */
      if (!window.__subsWarned) {
        window.__subsWarned = 1;
        toast(uiT('subsErr'), 'err', 5000);
      }
    }
  });

  /* ════════ 信息图标 / 收件箱：我提交的游戏 + 管理员的回复（网站 api/game_requests.php）════════ */
  (function(){

           inboxNewReply:'Neue Antwort auf deinen Spielwunsch', inboxLoading:'Wird geladen…',
           reqSub:'Sag uns, welches Spiel du willst. Die Antwort erscheint unter dem Info-Symbol neben deinem Avatar.' },
      ja:{ inboxTitle:'メッセージ', inboxSub:'リクエストしたゲームとチームからの返信です。',
           inboxEmpty:'まだメッセージはありません。送信したリクエストと返信がここに表示されます。',
           inboxWaiting:'返信待ち…', inboxNew:'新着', inboxYouAsked:'このゲームをリクエストしました',
           inboxStPending:'保留中', inboxStReplied:'返信済み', inboxStAdded:'追加済み', inboxStDeclined:'見送り',
           inboxNewReply:'ゲームリクエストに新しい返信があります', inboxLoading:'読み込み中…',
           reqSub:'希望のゲームを教えてください。返信はアバター横の情報アイコンに表示されます。' },
      es:{ inboxTitle:'Mensajes', inboxSub:'Tus solicitudes de juegos y las respuestas del equipo.',

      tr:{ inboxTitle:'Mesajlar', inboxSub:'Oyun istekleriniz ve ekibin yanıtları.',
           inboxEmpty:'Henüz bir şey yok. Gönderdiğin istekler ve yanıtlarımız burada görünecek.',
           inboxWaiting:'Yanıt bekleniyor…', inboxNew:'YENİ', inboxYouAsked:'Bu oyunu istedin',
           inboxStPending:'Beklemede', inboxStReplied:'Yanıtlandı', inboxStAdded:'Eklendi', inboxStDeclined:'Reddedildi',

           reqSub:'Hangi oyunu istediğini yaz. Yanıt, avatarının yanındaki bilgi simgesinde görünecek.' },
      pt:{ inboxTitle:'Mensagens', inboxSub:'Seus pedidos de jogos e as respostas da equipe.',
           inboxEmpty:'Nada por aqui ainda. Seus pedidos e nossas respostas aparecerão aqui.',
           inboxWaiting:'Aguardando resposta…', inboxNew:'NOVO', inboxYouAsked:'Você pediu este jogo',
           inboxStPending:'Pendente', inboxStReplied:'Respondido', inboxStAdded:'Adicionado', inboxStDeclined:'Recusado',
           inboxNewReply:'Nova resposta ao seu pedido de jogo', inboxLoading:'Carregando…',
           reqSub:'Diga qual jogo você quer. A resposta aparecerá no ícone de informações ao lado do seu avatar.' }
    };
    for (var c in X) {
      if (!UI_T[c]) { UI_T[c] = {}; }
      for (var k in X[c]) { UI_T[c][k] = X[c][k]; }
    }

    /* Announcements 弹窗文案 */
    var ANN_T = {
      en:{ annTitle:'Announcements', annSub:'Latest news from the team', annNew:'NEW',
           annEmpty:'No announcements yet.' },
      zh:{ annTitle:'公告', annSub:'来自团队的最新消息', annNew:'新',
           annEmpty:'暂时没有公告。' },
      ru:{ annTitle:'Объявления', annSub:'Последние новости от команды', annNew:'НОВОЕ',
           annEmpty:'Объявлений пока нет.' },
      de:{ annTitle:'Ankündigungen', annSub:'Neueste Neuigkeiten vom Team', annNew:'NEU',
           annEmpty:'Noch keine Ankündigungen.' },
      ja:{ annTitle:'お知らせ', annSub:'チームからの最新情報', annNew:'新着',
           annEmpty:'お知らせはまだありません。' },
      es:{ annTitle:'Anuncios', annSub:'Últimas noticias del equipo', annNew:'NUEVO',
           annEmpty:'Aún no hay anuncios.' },
      tr:{ annTitle:'Duyurular', annSub:'Ekibimizden son haberler', annNew:'YENİ',
           annEmpty:'Henüz duyuru yok.' },
      pt:{ annTitle:'Anúncios', annSub:'Últimas notícias da equipe', annNew:'NOVO',
           annEmpty:'Ainda não há anúncios.' }
    };
    for (var c2 in ANN_T) {
      if (!UI_T[c2]) { UI_T[c2] = {}; }
      for (var k2 in ANN_T[c2]) { UI_T[c2][k2] = ANN_T[c2][k2]; }
    }
  })();

  var INBOX = null;          /* null = 还没拉到；[] = 没有任何请求 */
  var INBOX_LAST = -1;       /* 上一次看到的未读数（-1 = 还没拉过） */
    if (typeof ANNOUNCEMENTS === 'undefined') { var ANNOUNCEMENTS = []; }
  function inboxEl(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
  function inboxIsOpen(){
    var m = inboxEl('inboxModal');
    return !!m && m.classList.contains('open');
  }
  function inboxSetBadge(n){
    var b = inboxEl('tbInboxBadge');
    if (!b) return;
    if (n > 0) { b.textContent = n > 9 ? '9+' : String(n); b.hidden = false; }
    else { b.hidden = true; }
  }
  function inboxRender(){
    var list = inboxEl('inboxList'), empty = inboxEl('inboxEmpty');
    if (!list || !empty) return;
    if (INBOX === null || !INBOX.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.textContent = uiT(INBOX === null ? 'inboxLoading' : 'inboxEmpty');
      empty.hidden = false;
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var h = '';
    INBOX.forEach(function(r){
      var st = /^(pending|replied|added|declined)$/.test(r.status) ? r.status : 'pending';
      h += '<div class="ib-item">'
        + '<div class="ib-head"><span class="ib-game">' + uiEsc(r.game) + '</span>'
        + '<span class="ib-st ib-st-' + st + '">'
        + uiEsc(uiT('inboxSt' + st.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + st.slice(1))) + '</span></div>'
        + '<div class="ib-meta">' + uiEsc(uiT('inboxYouAsked')) + ' · ' + uiEsc(r.date) + '</div>';
      var rp = r.replies || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < rp.length; i++) {
        h += '<div class="ib-reply' + (rp[i].unread ? ' is-new' : '') + '">'
          + '<div class="ib-reply-meta">' + uiEsc(rp[i].admin) + ' · ' + uiEsc(rp[i].date)
          + (rp[i].unread ? '<b>' + uiEsc(uiT('inboxNew')) + '</b>' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="ib-reply-body">' + uiEsc(rp[i].body) + '</div></div>';
      }
      if (!rp.length) { h += '<div class="ib-wait">' + uiEsc(uiT('inboxWaiting')) + '</div>'; }
      h += '</div>';
    });
    list.innerHTML = h;
  }
  function openInbox(){
    var m = inboxEl('inboxModal');
    if (!m) return;
    m.classList.add('open');
    inboxRender();
    wv('INBOX');
  }
  function closeInbox(){
    var m = inboxEl('inboxModal');
    if (m) m.classList.remove('open');
  }
  function inboxNewRequest(){
    closeInbox();
    openGameReq();
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    if (e.key === 'Escape') closeInbox();
  });
  window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', function(e){
    var msg = e.data;
    if (typeof msg !== 'string') return;

    if (msg.indexOf('ANNOUNCEMENTS_RAW:') === 0) {
      annClearWait();
      try {
        var annList = JSON.parse(msg.slice(18));
        if (annList && !Array.isArray(annList)) { annList = annList.announcements; }   /* 网站回 {ok:true, announcements:[...]} */
        if (Array.isArray(annList)) {
          ANNOUNCEMENTS = annList.map(function(a){
            return {
              id:    String(a.ann_key || a.id || ''),
              title: String(a.title || ''),
              text:  String(a.text || a.body || ''),
              date:  String(a.ann_date || a.date || '').slice(0, 10)
            };
          });
          ANN_STATE = 'ok';
          annRenderList();
          annPaintBadge();
        } else { ANN_STATE = 'err:FORMAT'; annRenderList(); }
      } catch (e) { ANN_STATE = 'err:PARSE'; annRenderList(); }
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('ANNOUNCEMENTS_ERR:') === 0) {
      annClearWait();
      ANN_STATE = 'err:' + msg.slice(18);
      annRenderList();
    }
    else if (msg.indexOf('INBOX_RAW:') === 0) {
      var d = null;
      try { d = JSON.parse(msg.slice(10)); } catch (err) {}
      if (!d || !d.ok) return;
      INBOX = d.requests || [];
      var un = d.unread | 0;
      if (inboxIsOpen()) {
        inboxRender();                         /* 这一次还带着「新」标记，让你看清哪些是新回复 */
        if (un > 0) { wv('INBOX_READ'); }      /* 同时告诉网站已读 */
        INBOX_LAST = 0; inboxSetBadge(0);
      } else {
        if (un > Math.max(INBOX_LAST, 0)) { toast(uiT('inboxNewReply'), 'info', 4500); }
        INBOX_LAST = un; inboxSetBadge(un);
      }
    }
    else if (msg === 'REQ_OK') {
      setTimeout(function(){ wv('INBOX'); }, 400);     /* 刚提交的请求马上出现在信息里 */
    }
  });
  setInterval(function(){ if (!document.hidden) { wv('INBOX'); wv('ANNOUNCEMENTS'); annWait(); } }, 60000);
  wv('INBOX');
  wv('ANNOUNCEMENTS');   /* 向宿主要公告列表；宿主回 ANNOUNCEMENTS_RAW:[...] */
  annWait();             /* 8 秒没回包就把「Loading…」换成超时提示，别一直转 */

  /* ════════ Announcements：顶部图标 + 弹窗 ════════
     公告数据就在这个数组里维护；有新的（id 没在 localStorage 里）时图标跳动 + 小红点。
     打开弹窗即视为已读。 */
  var ANN_STATE = 'wait';   /* wait = 还没收到宿主回复；ok；err:<代码> */
  var ANN_TIMER = null;
  var ANN_SEEN_KEY = 'starline.ann.seen';
  var ANN_SEEN = (function(){
    try {
      var raw = localStorage.getItem(ANN_SEEN_KEY);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    } catch (e) { return []; }
  })();
  function annIsUnread(a){ return ANN_SEEN.indexOf(a.id) < 0; }
  /* 等宿主回包：8 秒没动静就把状态从 wait 改成 err:TIMEOUT（避免界面一直显示 Loading…） */
  function annWait(){
    ANN_STATE = 'wait';
    if (ANN_TIMER) { clearTimeout(ANN_TIMER); }
    ANN_TIMER = setTimeout(function(){
      if (ANN_STATE === 'wait') { ANN_STATE = 'err:TIMEOUT'; annRenderList(); }
    }, 8000);
  }
  function annClearWait(){
    if (ANN_TIMER) { clearTimeout(ANN_TIMER); ANN_TIMER = null; }
  }
  function annUnreadCount(){
    var n = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < ANNOUNCEMENTS.length; i++) { if (annIsUnread(ANNOUNCEMENTS[i])) { n++; } }
    return n;
  }
  function annSaveSeen(){
    try { localStorage.setItem(ANN_SEEN_KEY, JSON.stringify(ANN_SEEN)); } catch (e) {}
  }
  function annPaintBadge(){
    var btn = document.getElementById('tbAnn');
    var dot = document.getElementById('tbAnnDot');
    var un  = annUnreadCount();
    if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('bounce', un > 0);
    if (dot) dot.hidden = !(un > 0);
  }
  function annRenderList(){
    var box = document.getElementById('annList');
    if (!box) return;
    if (!ANNOUNCEMENTS.length) {
      var emptyTxt = uiT('annEmpty');
      if (ANN_STATE === 'wait') { emptyTxt = 'Loading…'; }
      else if (ANN_STATE.indexOf('err:') === 0) { emptyTxt = 'Failed to load announcements (' + ANN_STATE.slice(4) + ')'; }
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="ann-item"><div class="ann-item-body">'
        + '<div class="ann-item-txt">' + uiEsc(emptyTxt) + '</div></div></div>';
      return;
    }
    var html = '';
    ANNOUNCEMENTS.forEach(function(a){
      var isNew = annIsUnread(a);
      html += '<div class="ann-item">'
        +   '<div class="ann-item-ico">'
        +     '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 11l18-8-8 18-2-8-8-2z"/></svg>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div class="ann-item-body">'
        +     '<div class="ann-item-top">'
        +       '<span class="ann-item-title">' + uiEsc(a.title || '') + '</span>'
        +       (isNew ? '<span class="ann-item-new">' + uiEsc(uiT('annNew')) + '</span>' : '')
        +     '</div>'
        +     '<div class="ann-item-txt">' + uiEsc(a.text || '') + '</div>'
        +     '<div class="ann-item-date">' + uiEsc(a.date || '') + '</div>'
        +   '</div>'
        + '</div>';
    });
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }
  /* 定位：贴在图标正下方，箭头对准图标中心 */
  function annPlace(){
    var btn = document.getElementById('tbAnn');
    var pop = document.getElementById('annPop');
    var arr = document.getElementById('annArrow');
    if (!btn || !pop) return;
    var r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
    var pw = pop.offsetWidth || 340;
    var ph = pop.offsetHeight || 260;
    var vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    var x = r.left + r.width / 2 - pw / 2;
    if (x + pw > vw - 10) x = vw - pw - 10;
    if (x < 10) x = 10;
    var y = r.bottom + 10;
    if (y + ph > vh - 10) { y = Math.max(10, r.top - ph - 10); }   /* 空间不够就翻到上面 */
    pop.style.left = Math.round(x) + 'px';
    pop.style.top  = Math.round(y) + 'px';
    if (arr) arr.style.left = Math.round(r.left + r.width / 2 - x) + 'px';
  }
  function openAnn(ev){
    if (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); ev.preventDefault(); }
    var pop = document.getElementById('annPop');
    if (!pop) return;
    annRenderList();
    annWait();
    wv('ANNOUNCEMENTS');                  /* 打开时再拉一次，保证看到最新公告 */
    pop.hidden = false;
    void pop.offsetWidth;                 /* 强制 reflow，让 transition 生效 */
    pop.classList.add('show');
    annPlace();
    /* 打开即视为已读：图标停止跳动、小红点消失 */
    ANNOUNCEMENTS.forEach(function(a){ if (annIsUnread(a)) { ANN_SEEN.push(a.id); } });
    annSaveSeen();
    setTimeout(annPaintBadge, 200);
  }
  function closeAnn(ev){
    if (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); ev.preventDefault(); }
    var pop = document.getElementById('annPop');
    if (!pop) return;
    pop.classList.remove('show');
    setTimeout(function(){ if (!pop.classList.contains('show')) { pop.hidden = true; } }, 220);
  }
  function toggleAnn(ev){
    var pop = document.getElementById('annPop');
    if (!pop) return;
    if (pop.hidden || !pop.classList.contains('show')) { openAnn(ev); }
    else { closeAnn(ev); }
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    var pop = document.getElementById('annPop');
    if (!pop || pop.hidden) return;
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#annPop') || t.closest('#tbAnn'))) return;
    closeAnn();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeAnn(); }
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){
    var pop = document.getElementById('annPop');
    if (pop && pop.classList.contains('show')) { annPlace(); }
  });

  /* 首次进页面初始化图标状态（有未读 → 跳动 + 小红点） */
  annPaintBadge();


  window.onRenewSuccess = function(){
    wv('GET_LICENSES');
  };

  applyUser();
  updateProfileGroup();       /* 个人资料里的实时组 + 管理员左边 tab */
  applyDPI(USER.dpi || 100);
  renderHero();
  if (typeof gpopBind === 'function') gpopBind();      /* 卡片悬停预览 / 列表右侧详情块 */
  renderGrid();
  renderQuickplay();
  renderDownloads();
  renderGenreBtn();
  renderGenrePop();
  startHeroTimer();
  if (typeof uiInit === 'function') uiInit();
  renderGenreBtn();     /* uiInit 里的 i18n 会重刷按钮文字，这里再校正一次 */
  wv('GET_LICENSES');
  wv('SYNC_SUBS');      /* 登录后拉一次服务端订阅，管理员冻结/删除会立刻反映到本地 */
  wv('MYINFO');         /* 顺手刷新一次「实时组」 */
  if (window.logoFx) logoFx.consumeLoginIntro();   /* 登录成功：3D Logo 飞到侧栏 */
  else document.body.classList.remove('logo-intro');
</script>

    var ld=0;function loop(now){if(!pl)return;t+=(now-last)/1000;last=now;if(t>=TT){t=TT;pl=false;ico();}if(now-ld>=33||!pl){ld=now;draw();}if(pl)raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}
    function play(){if(t>=TT)t=0;pl=true;last=performance.now();ico();cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}
    function pause(){pl=false;cancelAnimationFrame(raf);ico();}
    function seek(x){t=Math.max(0,Math.min(TT,x));ico();draw();}
    btn.onclick=function(){pl?pause():play();};

    draw();ico();play();
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(document.hidden)pause();});
    if(window.IntersectionObserver)new IntersectionObserver(function(es){if(!es[0].isIntersecting)pause();}).observe(vp);
    return {pause:pause,seek:seek,play:play,time:function(){return t;}};
  }
})();

    ja:{skGames:'すべてのゲーム',skNoMatch:'一致するゲームがありません',skTip:'閉じる',skBtnTitle:'メッセージ・FAQ・ゲーム（Ctrl+K）'},
    es:{skGames:'Todos los juegos',skNoMatch:'No hay juegos coincidentes',skTip:'cerrar',skBtnTitle:'Mensajes, FAQ y juegos (Ctrl+K)'},
    tr:{skGames:'Tüm oyunlar',skNoMatch:'Eşleşen oyun yok',skTip:'kapat',skBtnTitle:'Mesajlar, SSS ve oyunlar (Ctrl+K)'},
    pt:{skGames:'Todos os jogos',skNoMatch:'Nenhum jogo encontrado',skTip:'fechar',skBtnTitle:'Mensagens, FAQ e jogos (Ctrl+K)'}
  };
  for(var c in UI_T){var e=T[c]||T.en;for(var k in e)UI_T[c][k]=e[k];}

  function $(i){return document.getElementById(i);}
  var FQ=['faqQ1','faqQ2','faqQ3','faqQ4'];
  var box=document.querySelector('.topbar-search');
  var pop=$('skPop'),scroll=$('skScroll'),kbd=$('skKbd'),input=$('searchInput');
  var det=$('skDet'),detBody=$('skDetBody');
  if(!box||!pop||!scroll||!kbd||!input||!det||!detBody)return;
  /* 挂到 body 最外层 + fixed + 最高 z-index，不会再被任何界面压住 */
  document.body.appendChild(pop);document.body.appendChild(det);

  var CHEV='<svg class="sk-chev" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="9 18 15 12 9 6"/></svg>';
  var CARET='<svg class="sk-caret" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="9 18 15 12 9 6"/></svg>';
  var CHECK='<svg class="sk-own" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>';
  var REP='<svg class="sk-rep" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="9 14 4 9 9 4"/><path d="M20 20v-7a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H4"/></svg>';
  var QMARK='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M9.5 9.3a2.6 2.6 0 0 1 5 .9c0 1.7-2.5 2.2-2.5 3.9M12 17.2v.01"/></svg>';
  var MOON='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 12.79A9 9 0 1 1 11.21 3 7 7 0 0 0 21 12.79z"/></svg>';
  var SUN='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/><path d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M4.9 4.9l1.4 1.4M17.7 17.7l1.4 1.4M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.9 19.1l1.4-1.4M17.7 6.3l1.4-1.4"/></svg>';

  var secFolded=false;     /* 「消息」整块是否收起 */
  var openReq={};          /* 消息树里每个请求的展开状态（刷新后保留） */
  var activeRow=null;

  function esc(v){return uiEsc(v);}
  function inbox(){return (typeof INBOX!=='undefined'&&INBOX&&INBOX.length)?INBOX:[];}
  function stOf(r){return /^(pending|replied|added|declined)$/.test(r.status)?r.status:'pending';}
  function stText(st){return uiT('inboxSt'+st.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+st.slice(1));}
  function unreadOf(r){var n=0;(r.replies||[]).forEach(function(p){if(p.unread)n++;});return n;}
  function reqKey(r,i){return String(r.game)+'|'+String(r.date)+'|'+i;}
  function theme(){return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme')==='light'?'light':'dark';}

  function labels(){
    kbd.title=uiT('skBtnTitle');
    var tip=$('skTip');if(tip)tip.textContent=uiT('skTip');
    pop.setAttribute('aria-label',uiT('faqTitle'));
  }

  function gameIds(){
    var o=(typeof GRID_ORDER!=='undefined'&&GRID_ORDER&&GRID_ORDER.length)?GRID_ORDER:ORDER;
    return o.filter(function(g){return GAMES[g];});
  }

  /* ── 各区块 HTML ── */
  function themeHtml(){
    var t=theme();
    return '<div class="sk-sec"><button type="button" class="sk-item" data-act="theme" id="skTheme">'
      +'<i class="sk-ic">'+(t==='light'?SUN:MOON)+'</i><span class="sk-t">'+esc(uiT('toggleTheme'))+'</span>'
      +'<span class="sk-val">'+esc(uiT(t==='light'?'lightTheme':'darkTheme'))+'</span></button></div>';
  }

  function msgsHtml(){
    var list=inbox(),total=0;
    var h='<div class="sk-sec'+(secFolded?' sk-fold-on':'')+'" id="skMsgSec"><button type="button" class="sk-h sk-foldbtn" data-act="fold" aria-expanded="'+(!secFolded)+'">'+CARET+'<span>'+esc(uiT('inboxTitle'))+'</span>';
    list.forEach(function(r){total+=unreadOf(r);});
    h+='<b>'+(total?total+' '+esc(uiT('inboxNew')):list.length)+'</b></button><div class="sk-fold-body"><div class="sk-fold-in">';
    if(!list.length){
      h+='<div class="sk-none">'+esc(uiT(typeof INBOX!=='undefined'&&INBOX===null?'inboxLoading':'inboxEmpty'))+'</div></div></div></div>';
      return h;
    }
    list.forEach(function(r,i){
      var k=reqKey(r,i),un=unreadOf(r),rp=r.replies||[],st=stOf(r);
      var open=(k in openReq)?openReq[k]:un>0;
      h+='<div class="sk-grp'+(open?' open':'')+'" data-k="'+esc(k)+'">'
        +'<button type="button" class="sk-item sk-req" data-req="'+i+'">'+CARET
        +'<span class="sk-t sk-rg">'+esc(r.game)+'</span>'+(un?'<b class="sk-new">'+un+'</b>':'')
        +'<span class="sk-st sk-st-'+st+'">'+esc(stText(st))+'</span></button><div class="sk-kids">';
      rp.forEach(function(p,j){
        h+='<button type="button" class="sk-item sk-kid" data-req="'+i+'" data-rep="'+j+'">'+REP
          +'<span class="sk-t"><b>'+esc(p.admin)+'</b><em>'+esc(p.date)+'</em></span>'
          +(p.unread?'<b class="sk-newtag">'+esc(uiT('inboxNew'))+'</b>':'')+'</button>';
      });
      if(!rp.length)h+='<div class="sk-kid sk-wait">'+esc(uiT('inboxWaiting'))+'</div>';
      h+='</div></div>';
    });
    return h+'</div></div></div>';
  }

  function faqHtml(){
    var h='<div class="sk-sec"><div class="sk-h"><span>'+esc(uiT('faqTitle'))+'</span></div>';
    FQ.forEach(function(k,i){
      h+='<button type="button" class="sk-item sk-faq" data-faq="'+i+'"><i class="sk-ic">'+QMARK+'</i>'
        +'<span class="sk-t">'+esc(uiT(k))+'</span>'+CHEV+'</button>';
    });
    return h+'</div>';
  }

  function gamesHtml(){
    var ids=gameIds();
    var h='<div class="sk-sec"><div class="sk-h"><span>'+esc(uiT('skGames'))+'</span><b id="skCount">'+ids.length+'</b></div><div id="skGames">';
    ids.forEach(function(g){
      var m=GAMES[g],img=m.img||m.poster||'';
      h+='<button type="button" class="sk-item sk-game" data-game="'+esc(g)+'">'
        +'<span class="sk-th">'+(img?'<img src="'+esc(img)+'" alt="" loading="lazy" onerror="this.remove()">':'')+'</span>'
        +'<span class="sk-gt"><b>'+esc(m.title||g)+'</b><em>'+esc(m.dev||'')+'</em></span>'
        +(hasLic(g)?CHECK:CHEV)+'</button>';
    });
    return h+'</div><div class="sk-none" id="skNone" hidden>'+esc(uiT('skNoMatch'))+'</div></div>';
  }

  function build(){scroll.innerHTML=themeHtml()+msgsHtml()+faqHtml()+gamesHtml();}

  /* 搜索框里有字时，弹窗里的游戏列表同步过滤；清空就是全部游戏 */
  function filter(){
    var list=$('skGames');if(!list)return;
    var q=String(input.value||'').trim().toLowerCase(),n=0;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(list.children,function(b){
      var id=b.getAttribute('data-game'),m=GAMES[id]||{};
      var hit=!q||String(m.title||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0
                 ||String(m.dev||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0
                 ||String(id).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0;
      b.hidden=!hit;if(hit)n++;
    });
    var cnt=$('skCount');if(cnt)cnt.textContent=n;
    var none=$('skNone');if(none)none.hidden=n>0;
  }

  /* ── 消息详情（右侧带箭头的小弹窗） ── */
  function chip(st){return '<span class="sk-st sk-st-'+st+'">'+esc(stText(st))+'</span>';}
  function replyBlock(p){
    return '<div class="sk-rp'+(p.unread?' is-new':'')+'"><div class="sk-dm">'+esc(p.admin)+' · '+esc(p.date)
      +(p.unread?'<b class="sk-newtag">'+esc(uiT('inboxNew'))+'</b>':'')+'</div><div class="sk-db">'+esc(p.body)+'</div></div>';
  }
  function head(r){return '<div class="sk-dh"><span class="sk-dt">'+esc(r.game)+'</span>'+chip(stOf(r))+'</div>';}
  function reqDetail(r){
    var rp=r.replies||[],h=head(r)+'<div class="sk-dm">'+esc(uiT('inboxYouAsked'))+' · '+esc(r.date)+'</div>';
    if(!rp.length)h+='<div class="sk-db sk-dim">'+esc(uiT('inboxWaiting'))+'</div>';
    rp.forEach(function(p){h+=replyBlock(p);});
    return h;
  }
  function repDetail(r,p){return head(r)+replyBlock(p);}

  function placeDet(){
    if(!activeRow||det.hidden)return;
    var sr=scroll.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=activeRow.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(sr.height>0&&(rr.bottom<sr.top+4||rr.top>sr.bottom-4)){hideDet();return;}
    var pr=pop.getBoundingClientRect(),vw=document.documentElement.clientWidth,vh=document.documentElement.clientHeight;
    var w=det.offsetWidth,h=det.offsetHeight,gap=14;
    var roomR=vw-8-(pr.right+gap),roomL=pr.left-gap-8;
    var flip=roomR<w&&roomL>roomR;
    var left=flip?pr.left-gap-w:pr.right+gap;
    left=Math.max(8,Math.min(left,vw-8-w));
    var cy=rr.top+rr.height/2;
    var top=Math.max(8,Math.min(cy-26,vh-8-h));
    var ay=Math.max(14,Math.min(cy-top-6,h-26));
    det.style.left=Math.round(left)+'px';det.style.top=Math.round(top)+'px';
    det.style.setProperty('--sk-ay',Math.round(ay)+'px');
    det.classList.toggle('flip',flip);
  }
  function showDet(row,html){
    if(activeRow)activeRow.classList.remove('on');
    activeRow=row;row.classList.add('on');
    detBody.innerHTML=html;detBody.scrollTop=0;
    det.style.animation='none';det.hidden=false;void det.offsetWidth;det.style.animation='';
    placeDet();
  }
  function hideDet(){
    det.hidden=true;
    if(activeRow){activeRow.classList.remove('on');activeRow=null;}
  }

  /* ── 弹窗定位：fixed，箭头对准 Ctrl+K 按钮中心 ── */
  function place(){
    var br=kbd.getBoundingClientRect(),r=box.getBoundingClientRect();
    var vw=document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    var w=Math.min(440,vw-24);
    var left=Math.max(12,Math.min(r.right-w,vw-12-w));
    pop.style.width=w+'px';pop.style.left=Math.round(left)+'px';pop.style.top=Math.round(r.bottom+14)+'px';
    var ar=Math.max(16,Math.min(w-30,Math.round(left+w-(br.left+br.width/2)-6)));
    pop.style.setProperty('--sk-ar',ar+'px');
  }

  function blocked(){
    if(!box.offsetParent)return true;
    if(document.querySelector('.ui-view.open')||document.querySelector('.req-modal.open'))return true;
    var f=$('faqPop');if(f&&!f.hidden)return true;
    return false;
  }

  function open(){
    if(blocked())return;
    labels();build();filter();place();
    pop.hidden=false;kbd.classList.add('open');kbd.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
    scroll.scrollTop=0;
    if(typeof wv==='function'){try{wv('INBOX');}catch(e){}}   /* 顺手刷新一下消息 */
    try{input.focus();}catch(e){}
  }
  function close(){
    hideDet();
    if(pop.hidden)return;
    pop.hidden=true;kbd.classList.remove('open');kbd.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
  }
  window.skOpen=open;
  /* Esc：先收消息详情，再收主弹窗 */
  window.skClose=function(){if(!det.hidden){hideDet();return;}close();};

  function refreshTheme(){
    var b=$('skTheme');if(!b)return;
    var t=theme();
    b.querySelector('.sk-ic').innerHTML=(t==='light'?SUN:MOON);
    b.querySelector('.sk-val').textContent=uiT(t==='light'?'lightTheme':'darkTheme');
  }
  function refreshMsgs(){
    var sec=$('skMsgSec');if(!sec||pop.hidden)return;
    var tmp=document.createElement('div');tmp.innerHTML=msgsHtml();
    sec.parentNode.replaceChild(tmp.firstChild,sec);hideDet();
  }

  kbd.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();if(pop.hidden)open();else close();});

  scroll.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var fb=e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.sk-foldbtn'):null;
    if(fb){
      var sec=fb.parentNode;sec.classList.toggle('sk-fold-on');
      secFolded=sec.classList.contains('sk-fold-on');
      fb.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!secFolded));
      hideDet();return;
    }
    var b=e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.sk-item'):null;if(!b)return;
    var act=b.getAttribute('data-act');
    if(act==='theme'){
      if(typeof uiToggleTheme==='function')uiToggleTheme();
      refreshTheme();setTimeout(refreshTheme,80);setTimeout(refreshTheme,300);
      return;
    }
    var ri=b.getAttribute('data-req');
    if(ri!==null){
      var r=inbox()[parseInt(ri,10)];if(!r)return;
      var rep=b.getAttribute('data-rep');
      if(rep===null){
        var g=b.parentNode;g.classList.toggle('open');
        openReq[g.getAttribute('data-k')]=g.classList.contains('open');
        if(g.classList.contains('open'))showDet(b,reqDetail(r));else hideDet();
      }else{
        var p=(r.replies||[])[parseInt(rep,10)];if(!p)return;
        showDet(b,repDetail(r,p));
      }
      return;
    }
    var fi=b.getAttribute('data-faq'),gid=b.getAttribute('data-game');
    close();
    if(fi!==null&&typeof window.faqAsk==='function'){window.faqAsk(parseInt(fi,10));}
    else if(gid&&GAMES[gid]){openDetail(gid);}
  });
  scroll.addEventListener('scroll',placeDet);

  input.addEventListener('input',function(){if(!pop.hidden)filter();});

  document.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){
    if(pop.hidden)return;
    var t=e.target;
    if(box.contains(t)||pop.contains(t)||det.contains(t))return;
    close();
  },true);

  document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
    /* Ctrl+K / ⌘K：开关弹窗 */
    if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&!e.altKey&&!e.shiftKey&&(e.key==='k'||e.key==='K')){
      if(blocked()&&pop.hidden)return;
      e.preventDefault();
      if(pop.hidden)open();else close();
      return;
    }
    /* 弹窗开着时：上下键在条目间移动，回车触发 */
    if(pop.hidden)return;
    if(e.key==='ArrowDown'||e.key==='ArrowUp'){
      var items=Array.prototype.filter.call(pop.querySelectorAll('.sk-item'),function(b){return !b.hidden&&b.offsetParent;});
      if(!items.length)return;
      e.preventDefault();
      var i=items.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      i=(e.key==='ArrowDown')?(i+1)%items.length:(i<=0?items.length-1:i-1);
      items[i].focus();
      if(items[i].scrollIntoView)items[i].scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});
    }
  });

  window.addEventListener('resize',function(){if(!pop.hidden){place();placeDet();}});

  /* 宿主推来新的收件箱数据时，弹窗开着就刷新消息树 */
  try{
    if(window.chrome&&window.chrome.webview){
      window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message',function(ev){
        var m=ev.data;
        if(typeof m==='string'&&m.indexOf('INBOX_RAW:')===0&&!pop.hidden)setTimeout(refreshMsgs,0);
      });
    }
  }catch(err){}

  /* 语言切换时刷新文案 */
  var prevLang=window.onUiLangChanged;
  window.onUiLangChanged=function(){
    var r=typeof prevLang==='function'?prevLang.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    labels();
    if(!pop.hidden){var st=scroll.scrollTop;hideDet();build();filter();scroll.scrollTop=st;}
    return r;
  };
  labels();
})();
</script>
<script>
/* ═══════ 顶栏语言按钮：国旗 → 点击在下方弹出带箭头的语言列表（国旗 · 5px · 语言名称） ═══════ */
(function(){
  var btn=document.getElementById('tbLang'),pop=document.getElementById('lgPop');
  if(!btn||!pop||typeof UI_LANGS==='undefined')return;
  document.body.appendChild(pop);
  var CK='<svg class="lg-ck" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>';
  var PEN='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 3 3L7 19l-4 1 1-4z"/></svg>';
  var PLUS='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/></svg>';

  function cur(){return (typeof uiState!=='undefined'&&uiState.lang)||document.documentElement.lang||'en';}
  function find(code){for(var i=0;i<UI_LANGS.length;i++)if(UI_LANGS[i].code===code)return UI_LANGS[i];return null;}
  function flag(l){return '<span class="lg-flag">'+uiFlagSvg(l.flag)+'</span>';}

  function paintBtn(){
    var l=find(cur())||UI_LANGS[0];
    btn.innerHTML=flag(l);
    try{btn.title=uiT('language');}catch(e){}
  }
  function render(){
    var c=cur(),h='';
    UI_LANGS.forEach(function(l){
      h+='<button type="button" role="option" class="lg-item'+(l.code===c?' active':'')+'" data-lang="'+l.code+'" lang="'+l.code+'" aria-selected="'+(l.code===c)+'">'
        +flag(l)+'<span class="lg-name">'+uiEsc(l.native)+'</span>'
        +(l.custom?'<span class="lg-edit" role="button" data-edit="'+l.code+'" title="'+uiEsc(uiT('ctEdit'))+'">'+PEN+'</span>':'')
        +CK+'</button>';
    });
    h+='<div class="lg-sep"></div><button type="button" class="lg-item lg-add" data-add="1"><span class="lg-ico">'+PLUS+'</span><span class="lg-name">'+uiEsc(uiT('ctNew'))+'</span></button>';
    pop.innerHTML=h;
  }
  /* 箭头对准国旗按钮中心，弹窗在按钮正下方 */
  function place(){
    var br=btn.getBoundingClientRect(),vw=document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    var w=pop.offsetWidth,cx=br.left+br.width/2;
    var left=Math.max(12,Math.min(Math.round(cx-w/2),vw-12-w));
    pop.style.left=left+'px';pop.style.top=Math.round(br.bottom+12)+'px';
    pop.style.setProperty('--lg-ar',Math.max(14,Math.min(w-26,Math.round(cx-left-6)))+'px');
  }
  function open(){
    render();pop.hidden=false;place();
    btn.classList.add('open');btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
  }
  function close(){
    if(pop.hidden)return;
    pop.hidden=true;btn.classList.remove('open');btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
  }
  window.lgClose=close;

  btn.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();if(pop.hidden)open();else close();});
  pop.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var ed=e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.lg-edit'):null;
    if(ed){close();if(window.uiOpenTransEditor)uiOpenTransEditor(ed.getAttribute('data-edit'));return;}
    var ad=e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.lg-add'):null;
    if(ad){close();if(window.uiOpenTransEditor)uiOpenTransEditor();return;}
    var b=e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.lg-item'):null;if(!b)return;
    var code=b.getAttribute('data-lang');close();
    if(code&&code!==cur()&&typeof uiSetLang==='function')uiSetLang(code);
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){
    if(pop.hidden)return;
    var t=e.target;if(btn.contains(t)||pop.contains(t))return;
    close();
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
    if(pop.hidden||(e.key!=='ArrowDown'&&e.key!=='ArrowUp'))return;
    var items=Array.prototype.slice.call(pop.querySelectorAll('.lg-item'));if(!items.length)return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var i=items.indexOf(document.activeElement);
    i=(e.key==='ArrowDown')?(i+1)%items.length:(i<=0?items.length-1:i-1);
    items[i].focus();
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize',function(){if(!pop.hidden)place();});

  /* 语言变化（含设置页、宿主推送）时同步按钮上的国旗和列表选中态 */
  var prev=window.onUiLangChanged;
  window.onUiLangChanged=function(){
    var r=typeof prev==='function'?prev.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    paintBtn();if(!pop.hidden){render();place();}
    return r;
  };
  paintBtn();
})();
</script>
<script>
/* ═══════ 右下角拖拽手柄：拖动即改变 APP 窗口大小（向宿主发 RESIZE:宽x高） ═══════ */
(function(){
  if(document.getElementById('uiResizeGrip'))return;
  var g=document.createElement('div');
  g.id='uiResizeGrip';g.setAttribute('data-nodrag','');g.setAttribute('role','separator');g.setAttribute('aria-label','Resize');
  g.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 12 12"><path d="M11 3L3 11M11 7L7 11"/></svg>';
  document.body.appendChild(g);

  var drag=null,raf=0,pend=null,last=null,root=document.documentElement;

  function flush(){
    raf=0;
    var geom=window.uiWinGeom;if(!drag||!pend||!geom)return;
    var mn=geom.min(),mx=geom.max();
    /* 窗口左上角固定：让右下角始终跟在鼠标下面（按下时的偏移保持不变） */
    var w=Math.round((pend.x+drag.ox)*drag.scale),h=Math.round((pend.y+drag.oy)*drag.scale);
    w=Math.max(mn[0],Math.min(mx[0],w));h=Math.max(mn[1],Math.min(mx[1],h));
    if(last&&last.w===w&&last.h===h)return;
    last={w:w,h:h};geom.send(w,h);
  }
  function end(){
    if(!drag)return;
    drag=null;pend=null;last=null;root.classList.remove('ui-resizing');
  }

  g.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    if(e.button!==0||!window.uiWinGeom)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    drag={ox:window.innerWidth-e.clientX,oy:window.innerHeight-e.clientY,scale:window.uiWinGeom.scale()};
    try{g.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
    root.classList.add('ui-resizing');
  });
  g.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){
    if(!drag)return;
    pend={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};
    if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(flush);
  });
  g.addEventListener('pointerup',end);
  g.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
  g.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',end);
  /* 双击手柄：恢复为当前缩放对应的默认窗口大小 */
  g.addEventListener('dblclick',function(e){e.preventDefault();if(window.uiWinGeom)window.uiWinGeom.reset();});
})();
</script>

        ctCopy:'Copiar',ctCopied:'Copiado',ctDownload:'Baixar',ctImportFile:'Importar arquivo…',ctApply:'Aplicar ao editor',ctTemplate:'Incluir todos os textos como modelo (com original em inglês)',

  for(var c2 in T2){for(var k2 in T2[c2])T[c2][k2]=T2[c2][k2];}
  for(var c in T){if(!UI_T[c])UI_T[c]={};for(var k in T[c])UI_T[c][k]=T[c][k];}
  /* 其他脚本启动时会往所有语言表里批量写英文，这里把自定义语言恢复回来 */
  uiCustomReapply();
  if(String(uiState.lang).indexOf('x-')===0){
    try{uiRenderI18n();uiRenderLangGrid();}catch(e){}
    if(typeof onUiLangChanged==='function')onUiLangChanged(uiState.lang);
  }
  var sb=document.getElementById('uiTransBtn');if(sb)sb.hidden=false;

  var root=null,E={},editId=null,draft={},orig='',keys=[],rows=[],shown=0,CHUNK=60,delArm=0,escArm=0;
  function t(k){return uiT(k);}
  function flagOf(code){return '<span class="ct-flag">'+uiFlagSvg(code)+'</span>';}

  function build(){
    root=document.createElement('div');root.className='ct-view';root.id='ctView';root.hidden=true;
    root.innerHTML=
      '<div class="ct-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">'
      +'<div class="ct-top"><div class="ct-title" id="ctTitle"></div>'
      +'<button type="button" class="ct-x" id="ctArchBtn"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M14 3H7a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V8z"/><path d="M14 3v5h5"/><line x1="9" y1="13" x2="15" y2="13"/><line x1="9" y1="17" x2="15" y2="17"/></svg></button>'
      +'<button type="button" class="ct-x" id="ctX"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><line x1="6" y1="6" x2="18" y2="18"/><line x1="18" y1="6" x2="6" y2="18"/></svg></button></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-meta">'
      +'<label class="ct-fld"><span id="ctLFlag"></span><span class="ct-flagrow"><input class="ct-in" id="ctFlag" maxlength="6" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="vn"><span class="ct-prev" id="ctPrev"></span></span></label>'
      +'<label class="ct-fld ct-grow"><span id="ctLName"></span><input class="ct-in" id="ctName" maxlength="24" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>'
      +'<div class="ct-chip" id="ctChip"></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-stat" id="ctStat"></div></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-tools"><input class="ct-in" id="ctSearch" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off">'
      +'<label class="ct-chk"><input type="checkbox" id="ctOnly"><span id="ctLOnly"></span></label></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-cols"><div id="ctLCur"></div><div id="ctLYours"></div></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-list" id="ctList"></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-foot"><button type="button" class="ct-btn danger" id="ctDel" hidden></button>'
      +'<span class="ct-hint" id="ctMsg"></span>'
      +'<button type="button" class="ct-btn pri" id="ctSave"></button></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-arch" id="ctArch" hidden>'
      +'<div class="ct-top"><div class="ct-title" id="ctAT"></div><button type="button" class="ct-btn" id="ctABack"></button></div>'
      +'<div class="ct-abody"><div class="ct-asub" id="ctAS"></div>'
      +'<textarea class="ct-ta ct-json" id="ctATa" spellcheck="false"></textarea>'
      +'<label class="ct-chk"><input type="checkbox" id="ctATpl"><span id="ctALTpl"></span></label>'
      +'<div class="ct-aact"><button type="button" class="ct-btn" id="ctACopy"></button><button type="button" class="ct-btn" id="ctADl"></button>'
      +'<button type="button" class="ct-btn" id="ctAImp"></button><span class="ct-hint" id="ctAMsg"></span>'
      +'<button type="button" class="ct-btn pri" id="ctAApply"></button></div>'
      +'<input type="file" id="ctAFile" accept=".json,application/json,text/plain" hidden></div></div>'
      +'</div>';
    document.body.appendChild(root);
    ['ctTitle','ctX','ctLFlag','ctLName','ctFlag','ctPrev','ctName','ctChip','ctStat','ctSearch','ctOnly','ctLOnly','ctLCur','ctLYours','ctList','ctDel','ctMsg','ctSave','ctArchBtn','ctArch','ctAT','ctABack','ctAS','ctATa','ctATpl','ctALTpl','ctACopy','ctADl','ctAImp','ctAMsg','ctAApply','ctAFile'].forEach(function(i){E[i]=document.getElementById(i);});

    E.ctX.addEventListener('click',function(){close(false);});
    root.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){if(e.target===root)close(false);});
    E.ctFlag.addEventListener('input',function(){
      var v=E.ctFlag.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g,'');
      if(v!==E.ctFlag.value)E.ctFlag.value=v;
      preview();E.ctMsg.className='ct-hint';E.ctMsg.textContent=t('ctHint');
    });
    E.ctName.addEventListener('input',function(){preview();});
    E.ctSearch.addEventListener('input',function(){renderRows(true);});
    E.ctOnly.addEventListener('change',function(){renderRows(true);});
    E.ctList.addEventListener('input',function(e){
      var ta=e.target.closest?e.target.closest('textarea'):null;if(!ta)return;
      var k=ta.getAttribute('data-k');
      if(ta.value.trim())draft[k]=ta.value;else delete draft[k];
      stat();
    });
    E.ctList.addEventListener('scroll',function(){
      var l=E.ctList;
      if(shown<rows.length&&l.scrollTop+l.clientHeight>=l.scrollHeight-240)appendRows();
    });
    E.ctSave.addEventListener('click',save);
    E.ctDel.addEventListener('click',function(){
      if(!editId)return;
      if(Date.now()-delArm>3000){delArm=Date.now();E.ctDel.classList.add('arm');E.ctDel.textContent=t('ctDelSure');return;}
      uiCustomRemove(editId);close(true);
    });

    /* ── 翻译档案面板 ── */
    E.ctArchBtn.addEventListener('click',archShow);
    E.ctABack.addEventListener('click',archHide);
    E.ctATpl.addEventListener('change',archFill);
    E.ctACopy.addEventListener('click',archCopy);
    E.ctADl.addEventListener('click',archDownload);
    E.ctAImp.addEventListener('click',function(){E.ctAFile.value='';E.ctAFile.click();});
    E.ctAFile.addEventListener('change',function(){
      var f=E.ctAFile.files&&E.ctAFile.files[0];if(!f)return;
      var r=new FileReader();
      r.onload=function(){E.ctATa.value=String(r.result||'');archApply();};
      r.readAsText(f,'utf-8');
    });
    E.ctAApply.addEventListener('click',archApply);
  }

  function archMsg(txt,err){E.ctAMsg.className=err?'ct-err':'ct-hint';E.ctAMsg.textContent=txt||'';}
  function archJson(){
    var tpl=E.ctATpl.checked,tr={},src={};
    keys.forEach(function(k){
      if(draft[k])tr[k]=draft[k];else if(tpl)tr[k]='';
      if(tpl)src[k]=UI_T.en[k];
    });
    var o={format:'starline-lang-1',flag:E.ctFlag.value.trim().toLowerCase(),name:E.ctName.value.trim(),translations:tr};
    if(tpl)o.source=src;
    return JSON.stringify(o,null,2);
  }
  function archFill(){E.ctATa.value=archJson();archMsg('');}
  function archShow(){
    E.ctAT.textContent=t('ctArch');E.ctAS.textContent=t('ctArchSub');E.ctALTpl.textContent=t('ctTemplate');
    E.ctABack.textContent=t('ctBack');E.ctACopy.textContent=t('ctCopy');E.ctADl.textContent=t('ctDownload');
    E.ctAImp.textContent=t('ctImportFile');E.ctAApply.textContent=t('ctApply');
    E.ctATpl.checked=false;archFill();E.ctArch.hidden=false;
  }
  function archHide(){E.ctArch.hidden=true;}
  function archCopy(){
    var txt=E.ctATa.value;
    function ok(){archMsg(t('ctCopied'));}
    function fb(){try{E.ctATa.focus();E.ctATa.select();document.execCommand('copy');ok();}catch(e){}}
    try{if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(ok,fb);return;}}catch(e){}
    fb();
  }
  function archDownload(){
    try{
      var blob=new Blob([E.ctATa.value],{type:'application/json'});
      var a=document.createElement('a');
      var nm=(E.ctName.value.trim()||E.ctFlag.value.trim()||'translation').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_');
      a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=nm+'.json';
      document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
      setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);if(a.parentNode)a.parentNode.removeChild(a);},1500);
    }catch(e){}
  }
  function archApply(){
    var o;
    try{o=JSON.parse(E.ctATa.value);}catch(e){archMsg(t('ctArchErr'),true);return;}
    if(!o||typeof o!=='object'||Array.isArray(o)){archMsg(t('ctArchErr'),true);return;}
    var tr=(o.translations&&typeof o.translations==='object')?o.translations:(o.format?{}:o);
    var allowed={},nd={},cnt=0;keys.forEach(function(k){allowed[k]=1;});
    for(var k in tr){if(allowed[k]&&typeof tr[k]==='string'&&tr[k].trim()){nd[k]=tr[k];cnt++;}}
    var f=typeof o.flag==='string'?o.flag.trim().toLowerCase():'',n=typeof o.name==='string'?o.name.trim():'';
    if(!cnt&&!UI_FLAG_RE.test(f)&&!n){archMsg(t('ctArchErr'),true);return;}
    draft=nd;
    if(UI_FLAG_RE.test(f))E.ctFlag.value=f;
    if(n)E.ctName.value=n.slice(0,24);
    preview();stat();renderRows(true);archHide();
    E.ctMsg.className='ct-hint';E.ctMsg.textContent=t('ctArchOk')+' '+cnt;
  }

  function preview(){
    var f=E.ctFlag.value.trim().toLowerCase(),ok=UI_FLAG_RE.test(f),n=E.ctName.value.trim();
    E.ctPrev.innerHTML=ok?flagOf(f):'<span class="ct-flag ct-flag-empty"></span>';
    E.ctChip.innerHTML=(ok?flagOf(f):'<span class="ct-flag ct-flag-empty"></span>')+'<span>'+uiEsc(n||'—')+'</span>';
  }
  function stat(){
    var n=0;keys.forEach(function(k){if(draft[k])n++;});
    E.ctStat.textContent=n+' / '+keys.length+' '+t('ctDone');
  }
  function snap(){return JSON.stringify([E.ctFlag.value.trim(),E.ctName.value.trim(),draft]);}
  function dirty(){return snap()!==orig;}

  function rowHtml(k){
    var cur=uiT(k),rws=Math.max(1,Math.min(6,Math.ceil(cur.length/38)));
    return '<div class="ct-row"><div class="ct-l"><div class="ct-lt">'+uiEsc(cur)+'</div><code class="ct-k">'+uiEsc(k)+'</code></div>'
      +'<div><textarea class="ct-ta" data-k="'+uiEsc(k)+'" rows="'+rws+'" spellcheck="false" placeholder="'+uiEsc(t('ctTypePh'))+'">'+uiEsc(draft[k]||'')+'</textarea></div></div>';
  }
  function appendRows(){
    var end=Math.min(rows.length,shown+CHUNK),h='';
    for(var i=shown;i<end;i++)h+=rowHtml(rows[i]);
    shown=end;E.ctList.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h);
  }
  function renderRows(reset){
    var q=E.ctSearch.value.trim().toLowerCase(),only=E.ctOnly.checked;
    rows=keys.filter(function(k){
      if(only&&draft[k])return false;
      if(!q)return true;
      return k.toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0||uiT(k).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0||(draft[k]||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0;
    });
    E.ctList.innerHTML='';shown=0;if(reset)E.ctList.scrollTop=0;
    if(!rows.length){E.ctList.innerHTML='<div class="ct-none">'+uiEsc(t('ctNone'))+'</div>';return;}
    appendRows();
    /* 第一批不够撑满滚动区时继续补，保证能滚动加载 */
    while(shown<rows.length&&E.ctList.scrollHeight<=E.ctList.clientHeight+40)appendRows();
  }

  function labels(){
    E.ctTitle.textContent=t('ctTitle');E.ctArchBtn.title=t('ctArch');E.ctLFlag.textContent=t('ctFlag');E.ctLName.textContent=t('ctName');
    E.ctName.placeholder=t('ctNamePh');E.ctSearch.placeholder=t('ctSearch');E.ctLOnly.textContent=t('ctOnly');
    E.ctLCur.textContent=t('ctCurrent');E.ctLYours.textContent=t('ctYours');
    E.ctSave.textContent=t('ctSave');E.ctDel.textContent=t('ctDelete');E.ctDel.classList.remove('arm');
    E.ctMsg.className='ct-hint';E.ctMsg.textContent=t('ctHint');
  }

  function open(id){
    if(!root)build();
    if(typeof window.lgClose==='function')window.lgClose();
    if(typeof window.skClose==='function'){window.skClose();window.skClose();}
    var c=id?uiCustomFind(id):null;editId=c?c.id:null;
    draft={};if(c)for(var k in c.map)draft[k]=c.map[k];
    keys=Object.keys(UI_T.en).filter(function(k){return typeof UI_T.en[k]==='string'&&UI_T.en[k]!=='';});
    labels();E.ctArch.hidden=true;
    E.ctFlag.value=c?c.flag:'';E.ctName.value=c?c.native:'';
    E.ctSearch.value='';E.ctOnly.checked=false;
    E.ctDel.hidden=!c;delArm=0;escArm=0;
    preview();stat();renderRows(true);
    orig=snap();root.hidden=false;
    try{(c?E.ctName:E.ctFlag).focus();}catch(e){}
  }
  function close(force){
    if(!root||root.hidden)return;
    if(!force&&dirty()&&Date.now()-escArm>3000){
      escArm=Date.now();E.ctMsg.className='ct-err';E.ctMsg.textContent=t('ctDirty');return;
    }
    root.hidden=true;
  }
  function save(){
    var f=E.ctFlag.value.trim().toLowerCase(),n=E.ctName.value.trim();
    if(!UI_FLAG_RE.test(f)){E.ctMsg.className='ct-err';E.ctMsg.textContent=t('ctErrFlag');E.ctFlag.focus();return;}
    if(!n){E.ctMsg.className='ct-err';E.ctMsg.textContent=t('ctErrName');E.ctName.focus();return;}
    var saved=uiCustomUpsert({id:editId||uiCustomNewId(f),flag:f,native:n,map:draft});
    if(!saved)return;
    uiSetLang(saved.id);          /* 立刻切换到刚保存的语言，译文马上生效 */
    close(true);
  }
  window.uiOpenTransEditor=open;
  window.ctClose=function(){if(root&&E.ctArch&&!E.ctArch.hidden){archHide();return;}close(false);};
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
